# MATLAB to Python: functions and parameters

Generated by `python tools/gen_mapping.py` from the parameter registry
(`petakit5d.params`); do not edit it by hand. CI fails when it is out of date.

- **registered**: the port exists; its spec gives the Python function, the MATLAB-name
  function `petakit5d.matlab.<MATLAB name>` and the command line (`pk5d-master <MATLAB name>`).
- **planned**: the name the port will have (PLAN.md section 3.3), for review.
- Parameters keep MATLAB's names in snake_case (`xyPixelSize` -> `xy_pixel_size`,
  `DSRDirName` -> `dsr_dir_name`, `save16bit` -> `save_16bit`); the MATLAB layer takes the
  MATLAB names in any case and unique prefixes, as inputParser does.

60 registered, 41 planned, 23 not ported.

## Parameters

Arg: req (required, positional), opt (optional, positional), par (name-value; keyword-only in Python), pos (positional; MATLAB fills defaults by nargin). Conversion: the parameter's kind (params/kinds.py: the MATLAB-name layer, the command line and parameter files convert MATLAB's value to the Python one and back; empty: none; '<task> decides': open). The MATLAB default in MATLAB syntax, the Python default as the snake_case function takes it. Registered functions show their spec; planned ones MATLAB's inputParser or arguments block under the naming rule and the planned kinds (params/planned.py).

### `compute_tile_bwdist` -> `petakit5d.stitch.distance.compute_tile_bwdist`

``compute_tile_bwdist``: the distance map of tile ``tile_ind`` of the block information (``tile_distance_map`` with its overlap regions), written as a native single zarr (chunks ``block_size``, zstd; the separator ``/`` above 10,000 chunks) under a temporary name and renamed to ``bwdist_fullpath``. Nothing is done if that exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Block information file (stitching). |
| `tileInd` | `tile_ind` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isscalar` | The tile (0-based) in the block information. |
| `bwdistFullpath` | `bwdist_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Distance map to write (zarr). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Power of the weights (an integer). |
| `singleDistMap` | `single_dist_map` | req |  |  |  | `@islogical` | The map from the tile alone, not its overlap regions. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the map. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Not used (as in MATLAB: zstd). |
| `distBbox` | `dist_bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of full weight (None: none). |
| `dataOrderMat` | `data_order_axes` | req | custom:data_order_axes_from_matlab |  |  | `@(x) isvector(x)` | Array axes of x, y and z of the tile (stitch.geometry.parse_data_order). (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | opt |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace a map that appears while this one is computed. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the map and the zarr reads and writes (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs` -> `petakit5d.stitch.distance.compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs`

``compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs``: the map of tile ``tile_ind``'s z MIP slab (``<tile folder>/MIP_Slabs_pooling_<pool>/<name>_z.zarr``; ``tile_distance_map_mip_slab``), written as a native single zarr with ``shard_size`` and ``compressor`` under a temporary name and renamed to ``bwdist_fullpath``. Nothing is done if that exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Block information file (stitching). |
| `tileInd` | `tile_ind` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isscalar` | The tile (0-based) in the block information. |
| `bwdistFullpath` | `bwdist_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Distance map to write (zarr). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Power of the weights, applied in the blending. |
| `singleDistMap` | `single_dist_map` | req |  |  |  | `@islogical` | Not used (always the tile's own map). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the map. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Shard size (z, y, x) (None: none). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Compressor of the map. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Pool sizes (z, y, x) of the MIP slabs. |
| `distBbox` | `dist_bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of full weight in the tile. |
| `dataOrderMat` | `data_order_axes` | req | custom:data_order_axes_from_matlab |  |  | `@(x) isvector(x)` | Array axes of x, y and z of the tile (stitch.geometry.parse_data_order). (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | opt |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace a map that appears while this one is computed. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the map and the zarr reads and writes (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `createzarr` -> `petakit5d.io.create_zarr` (planned)

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `(1, 1) logical` |
| `dataSize` | `data_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500, 500, 500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500, 500, 500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par |  | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `char` |
| `order` | `order` | par | IO3 decides | `'F'` |  | `char` |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` |
| `groupWrite` | `group_write` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | par |  | `'zstd'` | `'zstd'` | `char` |
| `clevel` | `clevel` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `(1, 1) {mustBeNumeric}` |
| `zarrSubSize` | `zarr_sub_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` |
| `dimSeparator` | `dim_separator` | par |  | `'.'` | `'.'` | `char` |

### `decon_lucy_function` -> `petakit5d.decon.rl.decon_lucy`

Richardson-Lucy deconvolution with acceleration (decon_lucy_function): the result, err_mat, the number of iterations run and the output of ``overlap_fcn`` (see the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `I` | `i` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Data, a (z, y, x) volume or a (y, x) image. |
| `PSF` | `psf` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | PSF (z, y, x), at most the data's size. |
| `NUMIT` | `numit` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | custom:_background_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background subtracted from the data: a scalar ([]: 0), or one per voxel. |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | True: on a GPU, an error (decon:noUsableGPU) if none is usable; False: on the CPU; None: a GPU if one is usable, else the CPU with a warning. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Return uint16 (else single). |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor (> 1: results at most dampFactor x data). |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result. |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset added to the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the result to return (None: all). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Error metric and estimates of the iterations. |
| `debug_folder` | `debug_folder` | par |  | `'./debug/'` | `'./debug/'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the debug estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every saveStep iterations (debug). |
| `useMex` | `use_mex` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Iterations in the half-spectrum kernels: None where they can, True required, False MATLAB's loop on full spectra. |
| `overlapFcn` | `overlap_fcn` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x, 'function_handle')` | Function run after the iterations (on the GPU while they run); its output is returned. |

### `MIP_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_block`

``MIP_block``: for each batch (``batch_bboxes``, in the zarr), its MIP (or max pooling) along each axis written into that axis's slab zarr (``mip_fullpaths``, ``(z, y, x)``) at the batch's place relative to ``start_coord``; then the empty flag file. Pool sizes: 3 values along the MIP axis, 6 with the pooling across it, 9 with resampling (not ported). Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `MIPFullpaths` | `mip_fullpaths` | req | custom:_paths_from_matlab |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | MIP slab zarrs along z, y and x. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `startCoord` | `start_coord` | req | point1_yxz |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Start (z, y, x) of the region. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pool sizes (z, y, x): along the MIP axis[, across it[, resampling]]. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `omw_backprojector_generation` -> `petakit5d.decon.backprojector.omw_backprojector`

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `psf` | `psf` | req | volume |  |  |  | PSF, a (z, y, x) volume or a (y, x) image. |

| `skewed` | `skewed` | req |  |  |  |  | PSF in the skewed space; None (MATLAB's []): detected. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | Accumulated OTF energy quantile for the OTF mask. |
| `OTFAreaThresh` | `otf_area_thresh` | par |  | `100` | `100` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest component of the OTF mask, in voxels. |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && numel(x) == 2` | Apodization range of the relative distance to the mask's edge. |
| `minIntThrsh` | `min_int_thrsh` | par |  | `0.001` | `0.001` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Lower bound of the OTF threshold over DC. |

### `processFFCorrectionFrame` -> `petakit5d.flatfield.correction.flatfield_correct_file`

Flat-field correction of a frame in memory with the flat field and the background read from tiff files (``processFFCorrectionFrame``); the rest as ``flatfield_correct_volume``. The files are read at every call, as MATLAB reads them.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frame` | `frame` | req | volume |  |  |  | Data, (z, y, x) or (y, x). |
| `LSImage` | `ls_image` | req | same |  |  |  | Tiff file of the flat-field image (or stack). |
| `BackgroundImage` | `background_image` | req |  |  |  |  | Tiff file of the camera background image. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | req | same |  |  |  | If not empty, added after the correction instead of the background. |
| `LowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | opt |  | `0.4` | `0.4` |  | Lowest value of the normalized flat field. |
| `removeFFImBackground` | `remove_ff_im_background` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Subtract the background from the flat-field image. |
| `LSRescale` | `ls_rescale` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Divide the flat field by its maximum. |
| `castDataType` | `cast_data_type` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Cast the result to the data's class; otherwise single. |
| `out` | `out` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Array to write the result into (reused over frames). (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the correction (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `processStitchBlock` -> `petakit5d.stitch.block.process_stitch_block`

``processStitchBlock``: blend the batches ``batch_inds`` of the stitched volume (size ``im_size``, batches ``batch_size``) into ``stitch_fullname`` and write the task's flag (see the module). The batches' tiles come from ``per_block_info_fullname`` (JSON, or ``.mat``), else ``stitch_block_info`` (``{k: [TileBlock, ...]}`` for the task's k-th batch, MATLAB's struct); the tiles' paths from ``block_info_fullname``, else ``tile_fns``. Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based) of the task. |
| `BlockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The block information file (tile paths). |
| `PerBlockInfoFullname` | `per_block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The task's per-block information file. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `stitchFullname` | `stitch_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The stitched zarr (made before), written into. |
| `stitchBlockInfo` | `stitch_block_info` | opt | custom:_stitch_block_info_from_matlab | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isstruct(x)` | The batches' block information (without a per-block file). |
| `tileFns` | `tile_fns` | opt |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Tile zarr files (without a block file). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo a task whose flag exists. |
| `imSize` | `im_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Size (z, y, x) of the stitched volume. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par |  | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `@ischar` | Class of the stitched volume. |
| `BlendMethod` | `blend_method` | par |  | `'feather'` | `'feather'` | `@ischar` | 'none', 'max', 'mean', 'median' or 'feather'. |
| `BorderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Border of the batches (z, y, x). |
| `imdistFullpaths` | `imdist_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Distance maps (feather). |
| `imdistFileIdx` | `imdist_file_idx` | par | index1 | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | The distance map of each tile. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pooling of the distance maps (z, y, x). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | par |  | `10` | `10` | `@isnumeric` | Power of the distance weights (pooled maps). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the batches' z MIPs in the flag. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the kernels, reads and writes (None: the CPUs this process may use). (Python only) |

### `psf_gen_new` -> `petakit5d.decon.psf.psf_gen`

Clean, center and resample a raw PSF (psf_gen_new): the background removed, the peak at the FFT center ``size // 2``, z resampled to the data's step (see the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `psf` | `psf` | req | volume |  |  |  | Raw PSF, a (z, y, x) volume or a (y, x) image. |
| `dz_psf` | `dz_psf` | req |  |  |  |  | z step of the PSF. |
| `dz_data` | `dz_data` | req |  |  |  |  | z step of the data the PSF is resampled to. |
| `medFactor` | `med_factor` | opt |  | `1.5` | `1.5` |  | Factor of the median subtracted by the 'median' method. |
| `PSFGenMethod` | `psf_gen_method` | opt |  | `'masked'` | `'masked'` |  | Background removal: 'masked' or 'median'. |

### `resaveZarrBlock` -> `petakit5d.io.resave.resave_zarr_block`

Copy the input boxes ``batch_b_boxes`` of a zarr array into the output boxes ``region_b_boxes`` of an existing native array (``resaveZarrBlock``), then write the task's flag. The flag's folder must exist (else MATLAB's error: the run is over).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |

| `resultFullpath` | `result_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Output zarr (native, existing). |

| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_b_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Input box of each batch. |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_b_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Output box of each batch. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |

### `RLdecon` -> `petakit5d.decon.file.rl_decon_file`

Deconvolve a file (or ``rawdata``) and deskew and rotate the result (RLdecon): see the module. Arrays are (z, y, x); boxes, sizes and MIP axes in (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFn` | `input_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | Input file (tiff or zarr); '' for data in rawdata. |
| `outputFn` | `output_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | Result file; '' to only return the result. |
| `PSFfn` | `psf_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | PSF file (tiff, or a .mat file with the variable psf). (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `rawdata` | `rawdata` | par | volume | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Data (z, y, x) used instead of reading inputFn. |
| `nTapering` | `n_tapering` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Edge tapering in x-y (edgetaper; not ported). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Results in uint16 (else single). |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan data (rotation without deskew). |
| `Deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deskew the result. |
| `Rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Rotate the result (with the deskew for sample scans). |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deskew and rotate in one step. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the data in z first. |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation. |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Crop of the separate deskew. |
| `xStepThresh` | `x_step_thresh` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | x step above which the combined path interpolates. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background ([]: 0 for 'simplified' and 'omw'). |
| `Resample` | `resample` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Resample factor of the combined deskew and rotation (z, y, x). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'omw'` | `'omw'` | `@(x) any(strcmp(x, {'original', 'simplified', 'omw', 'cudagen'}))` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Fixed number of iterations (saved in the info file). |
| `errThresh` | `err_thresh` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Error threshold (saved in the info file). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save results as zarr. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Block size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `ErodeMaskfile` | `erode_maskfile` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Eroded mask zarr used instead of the erosion. |
| `deconBbox` | `decon_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the result. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the iterations' estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every saveStep iterations (debug). |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | True: on a GPU (an error if none is usable); False: the CPU; None: a GPU if one is usable. |
| `save3Dstack` | `save_3d_stack` | par | same | `[true,true,true]` | `(True, True, True)` | `@(x) islogical(x) && (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 3)` | Save the (decon, DS, DSR) stacks. (renamed) |
| `mipAxis` | `mip_axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && numel(x) == 3` | MIP axes (z, y, x). |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate the PSF (psf_gen) or use it as it is. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | uuid of the temporary names. |
| `overlapFcn` | `overlap_fcn` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x, 'function_handle')` | Function run on the CPU while the GPU deconvolves; its output is returned. |

### `RLdecon_for_zarr_block` -> `petakit5d.decon.block.decon_zarr_block`

``RLdecon_for_zarr_block``: deconvolve the batches of a large zarr file into the result zarr and write the task's flag (see the module). Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `psfFullpath` | `psf_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | PSF file. |
| `deconFullpath` | `decon_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch (with its border) in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region in the result. (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Result in uint16 (else single). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `-32.45` | `-32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background. |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of each batch's mask. |
| `maskFullpaths` | `mask_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | 2D masks (xy, xz, yz) of the regions to decon. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'simplified'` | `'simplified'` | `@ischar` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; the blocks fix the iterations (as in MATLAB). |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deconvolve on a GPU (an error if none is usable). |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate the PSF (psf_gen) or use it as it is. |

### `RLdecon_large_in_memory` -> `petakit5d.decon.large.decon_large_in_memory`

Deconvolve a file that fits in memory batch by batch (RLdecon_large_in_memory): see the module. Sizes are (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpaths` | `frame_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | File to deconvolve (tiff or zarr). |
| `deconFullpath` | `psf_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | PSF file (the second argument; MATLAB's inputParser names it deconFullpath). (renamed) |
| `psfFullpath` | `decon_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result file (the third argument; MATLAB's inputParser names it psfFullpath). (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Result in uint16 (else single). |
| `Rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (the batches are only deconvolved). |

| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the data in z first. |

| `EdgeSoften` | `edge_soften` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `zEdgeSoften` | `z_edge_soften` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |

| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `8` | `8` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `ErodeMaskfile` | `erode_maskfile` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Eroded mask tiff multiplied with the result instead. |
| `SaveMaskfile` | `save_maskfile` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the eroded mask (Masks/<name>_eroded.tif). |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |

| `maskFullpaths` | `mask_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | 2D masks (xy, xz, yz) of the regions to decon. |

### `RLdecon_large_in_place` -> `petakit5d.decon.large.decon_large_in_place`

Deconvolve a large zarr file in blocks, each written into the result (RLdecon_large_in_ place): see the module. Sizes are (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |

| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |

| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x), in whole chunks. |

### `saveMIP_tiff` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.save_mip_tiff`

The MIPs of a tiff file (or of its box) along the flagged axes, cast to ``dtype``, as ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` in MATLAB's orientation (``saveMIP_tiff``); nothing if ``mip_fullname`` exists. Each MIP is written under a temporary name and moved into place.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullname` | `frame_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Tiff file. |
| `MIPFullname` | `mip_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | MIP file name (<name>_MIP_z.tif); skipped if it exists. |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par |  | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `@ischar` | Class of the MIPs. |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |

### `saveMIP_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.save_mip_zarr`

The MIPs of a zarr file (or of its box) along the flagged axes, cast to ``dtype`` (the zarr's class if empty), as ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` in MATLAB's orientation (``saveMIP_zarr``). The image is read in z slabs of whole chunk layers (at least 64 chunks a read) with a running max along z, so it is not held whole. Each MIP is written under a temporary name and moved into place.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullname` | `zarr_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Zarr file. |
| `MIPFullname` | `mip_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | MIP file name (<name>_MIP_z.tif). |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x)` | Class of the MIPs (empty: the zarr's class). |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@(x) isvector(x) \|\| numel(x) == 3` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |

### `stitch_global_grid_assignment_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.stitch.assignment.global_grid_assignment`

The grid assignment of a group of tiles as a task: reads the group's problem from a .mat file (MATLAB's form: ``nF_g``, ``max_xcorr_mat_g``, ``absolute_shift_mat_g``, ``overlap_matrix_g``, ``max_allow_shift_g``, ``tileIdx_g``) and writes ``d_shift_g`` to ``output_fullpath`` (through a temporary file); an existing result is kept.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFullpath` | `input_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The group's optimization information (.mat). |
| `outputFullpath` | `output_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The result file (.mat, with d_shift_g). |
| `xcorr_thresh` | `xcorr_thresh` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isscalar(x)` | Pairs below this correlation are not fitted. |
| `axisWeight` | `axis_weight` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isvector(x)` | Weights of the neighbors along tile index z, y and x. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Id of the temporary result file. |

### `stitch_organize_block_info` -> `petakit5d.stitch.blockinfo.organize_block_info`

``stitch_organize_block_info``: the per-block information of batches ``block_inds[0]`` to ``block_inds[-1]`` (0-based) from the block information file, written as one JSON file per ``task_size`` batches (``stitch_block_info_blocks_<s>_<t>.json``, 1-based numbers; a temporary name, then a rename), then the task's flag (``blockInds``, ``t1``). Nothing is done if the flag exists (``overwrite`` removes it). Returns whether the task ran.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInds` | `block_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | First and last batch (0-based) of the task. |
| `taskSize` | `task_size` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batches per blending task (one JSON file each). |
| `BlockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The block information file. |
| `PerBlockInfoPath` | `per_block_info_path` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Folder of the per-block information files. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when the task is done. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo a task whose flag exists. |
| `BorderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Border of the batches (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary files (empty: a new one). |

### `tiffToZarr` -> `petakit5d.io.convert.tiff_to_zarr`

Convert a tiff file (its partial files joined along z, or ``frame``, a ``(z, y, x)`` array) to a native zarr array (``tiffToZarr``), with the flip in z, the crop boxes, resampling and the user function in MATLAB's order, and the result cast back to the input's class. An existing result is kept (``overwrite``: replaced first).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tifFilename` | `tif_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Tiff file, or its partial files. |
| `zarrFilename` | `zarr_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Zarr result. |
| `frame` | `frame` | opt | volume | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Data in memory to convert instead (None: read the tiff). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,250]` | `(250, 500, 500)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) (Zarr v3 with shards). |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Store an image as one z slice ([1, ny, nx]). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the volume in z. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling factors (z, y, x) or (z, xy) (None: none). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to read. |
| `tileOutBbox` | `tile_out_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to keep after the flip. |
| `readWholeTiff` | `read_whole_tiff` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Read the tiff at once (else by z blocks). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | par |  | `'zstd'` | `'zstd'` | `@ischar` | Zarr compressor. |
| `usrFcn` | `usr_fcn` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x)` | User function applied last (callable, import path or record). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary result (empty: a new one). |

### `unmix_channels_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_block`

``unmix_channels_block``: the linear unmixing of each batch (``batch_bboxes``) of the channels' zarr files, written into the same box of the result zarr ``unmix_fullpath`` in its class; then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpaths` | `zarr_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | Zarr files of the channels. |
| `unmixFullpath` | `unmix_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `unmix_channels_gaussian_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_gaussian_block`

``unmix_channels_gaussian_block``: each batch with its border (``batch_bboxes``), each channel with a nonzero sigma filtered (``imgaussfilt3`` in single), unmixed, its region (``local_bboxes``) written into ``region_bboxes`` of the result zarr in its class; then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpaths` | `zarr_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | Zarr files of the channels. |
| `unmixFullpath` | `unmix_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `unmixSigmas` | `unmix_sigmas` | req | custom:_sigmas_in |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Sigma of each channel: a scalar or (z, y, x); 0 for no filter. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch with its border. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region in the result. (renamed) |
| `localBBoxes` | `local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each region inside its batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` |

### `XR_crop_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_block`

``XR_crop_block``: each batch (``batch_bboxes``, boxes of the input zarr that may reach outside it: zeros there) written into its region (``region_bboxes``) of the result zarr ``crop_fullpath``; then the flag: empty, or with the MIPs of the blocks for MIP slabs of ``mip_batch_size`` (``save_block_mips``). Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``) or if the writer of the crop has ended (``writers.orphaned``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `cropFullpath` | `crop_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `XR_crop_dataset` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_dataset`

Crop every file of the datasets that matches a channel pattern (``XR_crop_dataset``) into ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.tif`` (or ``.zarr`` with ``save_zarr``; large zarr files always give zarr), one ``crop_file`` task per file through the job framework. ``crop_type``: ``'fixed'`` crops ``input_bbox``; ``'moving'`` moves it linearly from its start at the first time point to ``last_start_coords`` at the last (per channel, rounded as MATLAB rounds); ``'center'`` crops a box of its size at each image's center. parameters.mat of the call goes into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to crop. |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Cropped'` | `'Cropped'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `cropType` | `crop_type` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed' (the box), 'moving' (moved linearly to last_start_coords over the time points) or 'center' (a box of the box's size at the image's center). |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box outside the image. |
| `lastStartCoords` | `last_start_coords` | par | point1_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Start (z, y, x) of the box at the last time point ('moving'). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to crop. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr files: crop in blocks (crop_zarr). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the results as zarr. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_crop_frame` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_file`

Crop a tiff or zarr file with a box (``XR_crop_frame``) into ``save_fullpath`` (tiff, or zarr with ``save_zarr``, chunks ``block_size``). Without ``pad`` the box's end is cut at the image's end; with it, the part outside the image is ``pad_value`` (cast to the image's class). Large zarr files are cropped in blocks (``crop_zarr``). Nothing is done if the input is missing (a warning) or the result exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataFullpath` | `data_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input tiff or zarr file. |
| `saveFullpath` | `save_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Result file (.tif or .zarr). |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1); its end is cut at the image's end without pad. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box outside the image. |
| `padValue` | `pad_value` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Value of the padding. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The input is zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr input: crop in blocks (crop_zarr). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as zarr. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks of large files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_crop_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_zarr`

Crop a large zarr file in blocks (``XR_crop_zarr``): the batches of the box (``batch_size``, whole chunks of ``block_size``) as ``crop_block`` tasks through the job framework into ``<crop>_<uuid>``, its MIPs (``mip_zarr``, merged from the MIPs of the blocks), then the result moved into place. A box past the image's end needs ``pad``. Nothing is done if the result exists; a writer of another call of the same result is waited for, and a result another writer published is kept. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `cropFullpath` | `crop_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Result zarr. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to crop. |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box past the image's end. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of a block. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the MIPs of the result (MIPs/). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks on a local process pool. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_decon_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.decon.dataset.decon_dataset`

Deconvolve the datasets' files (XR_decon_data_wrapper): see the module. Sizes in (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folder(s). |

| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to deconvolve. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan step (um). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par | same | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@(x) isvector(x) && numel(x) <= 2` | Pixel size (um). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@(x) numel(x) == 1 && islogical(x)` | uint16 results. |
| `parseSettingFile` | `parse_setting_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip z stacks after their setting files. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the z stacks. |
| `background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background, or one per channel ([]: 100). |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSFs (um). |
| `edgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges after the decon (voxels). |
| `erodeByFTP` | `erode_by_ftp` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Erode with the mask of the first time point. |
| `psfFullpaths` | `psf_fullpaths` | par |  | `{'','',''}` | `('', '', '')` | `@iscell` | PSF files, in the order of channel_patterns. |
| `deconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Iterations, or one per dataset. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'simplified'` | `'simplified'` | `@ischar` | 'simplified', 'omw' or 'original'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha (OMW), or one per channel. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW), or one per channel. |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1.0)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | PSF in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the iterations' estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every save_step iterations (debug). |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate clean PSFs (psfgen/). |
| `GPUJob` | `gpu_job` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deconvolve on GPUs. |
| `deconRotate` | `decon_rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deskew and rotate the results (DSR/). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |

| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a file's job. |
| `taskBatchNum` | `task_batch_num` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Files per job (None: automatic for GPU jobs). |

### `XR_deskew_rotate_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.dataset.deskew_rotate_dataset`

Deskew and/or rotate the files of the datasets ``data_paths`` that match a channel pattern (``XR_deskew_rotate_data_wrapper``), one task per file through the job framework; see the module's description. Raises if files are still not done after the retries.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `DSDirName` | `ds_dir_name` | par |  | `'DS'` | `'DS'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the deskewed results under each data path. |
| `DSRDirName` | `dsr_dir_name` | par |  | `'DSR'` | `'DSR'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the deskewed and rotated results. |
| `deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deskew the data. |
| `rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Rotate the deskewed data. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Remove the result folders first. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0','CamB_ch1'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0', 'CamB_ch1')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to process. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan interval in um. |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@(x) isvector(x) && numel(x) <= 2` | Pixel size in um. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `zStageScan` | `z_stage_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Z stage scan (orthogonal to objective scan). |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the z stacks. |
| `parseSettingFile` | `parse_setting_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip each file's z stack as its setting file says. |
| `crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Crop the empty triangles of the separate deskew. |
| `inputAxisOrder` | `input_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the input. |
| `outputAxisOrder` | `output_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the output. |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Combined deskew, rotation and resampling. |
| `FFCorrection` | `ff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flat-field correction (regular files). |
| `BKRemoval` | `bk_removal` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Camera background removal (known issue: MATLAB errors). |
| `lowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | `@isnumeric` | Lowest value of the normalized flat field. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | If set, a constant background instead of the camera's. |
| `FFImagePaths` | `ff_image_paths` | par |  | `{'','',''}` | `('', '', '')` | `@iscell` | Flat-field images, one per channel pattern. |
| `backgroundPaths` | `background_paths` | par |  | `{'','',''}` | `('', '', '')` | `@iscell` | Background images, one per channel pattern. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save 16-bit results (else single). |
| `save3DStack` | `save_3d_stack` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the volumes (else empty result files). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the MIPs of the results. |

### `XR_deskewRotateBlock` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.zarr.deskew_rotate_block`

``XR_deskewRotateBlock``: each batch (rows of the input zarr, ``in_batch_bboxes``; flipped along z with ``flip_zstack``) deskewed and rotated on the output box its region has in the full result (``out_local_bboxes`` moved by ``out_batch_bboxes``' x and z starts), written into its region (``out_region_bboxes``) of the result zarr ``dsr_fullpath``; then the flag: ``t``, or with the MIPs of the blocks for MIP slabs of ``mip_batch_size``. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``) or if the writer of the result has ended (``writers.orphaned``). Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `dsrFullpath` | `dsr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `inBatchBBoxes` | `in_batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input (with its border rows). (renamed) |
| `outBatchBBoxes` | `out_batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the full result (with its border rows). (renamed) |
| `outRegionBBoxes` | `out_region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result zarr. (renamed) |
| `outLocalBboxes` | `out_local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region inside the batch. |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan interval in um. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip each batch along z after its read. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | uint16 result (else single). |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation: 'linear' or 'cubic'. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling after rotation (z, y, x). |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the reads, kernels and writes (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `XR_deskewRotateZarr` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.zarr.deskew_rotate_zarr`

Deskew and rotate a large zarr file in blocks (``XR_deskewRotateZarr``): the batches of output rows (``batch_size``'s y; all of x and z), with a border row on each side, as ``deskew_rotate_block`` tasks through the job framework (or a local pool of 12 workers with ``parse_parfor`` and no cluster) into ``<dir>/<result_dir_str>/<name>.zarr_<uuid>``, then its MIPs (``mip_zarr``, merged from the MIPs of the blocks) and the result moved into place. Sample scan only. Nothing is done if the result exists; a writer of another call of the same result is waited for, and a result another writer published is kept. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpath` | `frame_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input zarr (or a cell of one). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Scan interval in um. |
| `resultDirStr` | `result_dir_str` | par |  | `'DSR/'` | `'DSR/'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under the input's folder. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan (not supported: MATLAB's error). |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Accepted; not used (always combined). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the input along z (each batch after its read). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | uint16 result (else single). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the MIPs of the result (MIPs/). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (the result is zarr). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x); a batch is all of x and z. |

| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the input to deskew and rotate. |
| `taskSize` | `task_size` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Batches per task (empty: MATLAB's rule). |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling after rotation (z, y, x). |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation: 'linear' or 'cubic'. |

| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `8` | `8` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |

Nothing is done if the result exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Percentile of the MIP below which values enter Otsu. |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_block`

``XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_block`` (the first method; the zarr workflow doesn't use it): each batch thresholded on its Gaussian filter at Otsu's threshold of its values below their ``otsu_max_per`` percentile (bounded by ``min_threshold``, or ``0.75 * base_threshold + 0.25 * T``), small objects removed, written into its region; then the flag.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers. (renamed) |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `outFullpath` | `out_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file of the task. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result. (renamed) |
| `localBBoxes` | `local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each result region in its batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Percentile of the MIP below which values enter Otsu. |
| `MinThreshold` | `min_threshold` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest threshold. |
| `MaxThreshold` | `max_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Largest threshold (empty: none). |
| `BaseThreshold` | `base_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Base threshold weighed with the local one (empty: none). |
| `volThrsh` | `vol_thrsh` | par |  | `1000` | `1000` | `@isvector` | Objects smaller than this are removed. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Not used. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all). (Python only) |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_interpolated_background_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_interpolated_background_block`

``XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_interpolated_background_block``: as the updated method, with the threshold of each voxel from the estimated background (``bg_fullpath``, one value per background batch, enlarged linearly to the batch) times ``bg_factor``.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers. |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `outFullpath` | `out_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr. |
| `bgFullpath` | `bg_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Background zarr of the background estimation. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file of the task. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result. (renamed) |
| `localBBoxes` | `local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each result region in its batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `bgFactor` | `bg_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@isnumeric` | Factor of the background. |

| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Passed to the point detection (not used there). |
| `volThrsh` | `vol_thrsh` | par |  | `1000` | `1000` | `@isvector` | Objects smaller than this are removed. |
| `offset` | `offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Added to the input's nonzero voxels. |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_updated_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_updated_block`

``XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_updated_block``: each batch (``batch_bboxes`` in the input zarr) thresholded on its Gaussian filter (Otsu's threshold of the values below the MIP's ``otsu_max_per`` percentile, bounded by ``min_threshold``, or weighed with ``base_threshold`` and the MIP background mask's threshold), small objects removed, bright puncta removed by point detection, ``offset`` added, cast to the result's class and written into its region (``region_bboxes``, the batch's part ``local_bboxes``); then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers. |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `outFullpath` | `out_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file of the task. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result. (renamed) |
| `localBBoxes` | `local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each result region in its batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Percentile of the MIP below which values enter Otsu. |
| `MinThreshold` | `min_threshold` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest threshold. |
| `MaxThreshold` | `max_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Largest threshold (empty: none). |
| `BaseThreshold` | `base_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Base threshold weighed with the local ones (empty: none). |
| `volThrsh` | `vol_thrsh` | par |  | `1000` | `1000` | `@isvector` | Objects smaller than this are removed. |
| `offset` | `offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Added to the input's nonzero voxels. |
| `localWinSize` | `local_win_size` | par | size_yxz | `[15,15,23]` | `(23, 15, 15)` | `@isvector` | Window (z, y, x) of the point detection. |
| `SigmaThrsh` | `sigma_thrsh` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma thresholds of the point detection (one pass each). |
| `intThrsh` | `int_thrsh` | par |  | `10000` | `10000` | `@isnumeric` | Intensity thresholds of the point detection. |
| `initDetect` | `init_detect` | par |  | `'2d'` | `'2d'` | `@ischar` | Initial detection: '2d' or '3d'. |
| `detVolThrsh` | `det_vol_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest punctum volumes of the point detection. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all). (Python only) |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_zarr`

Puncta removal of a large zarr file in batches (``XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_zarr``): the batches (``batch_size`` with borders of 100, whole chunks of ``block_size``) as block tasks of ``mode`` through the job framework into ``<data>/Puncta_Removed/<name>.zarr_<uuid>``, its MIPs, then the result moved into place; with ``'bg_estimation'`` the thresholds of the batches into ``<data>/Puncta_background_estimation/<name>_batch_<y>_<x>_<z> _background_values.zarr`` instead. Nothing is done if the result exists. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'updated'` | `'updated'` | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | 'updated', 'bg_interpolated' (thresholds from bg_fullpath) or 'bg_estimation'. |
| `bgFullpath` | `bg_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Background zarr of 'bg_estimation' ('bg_interpolated'). |
| `bgFactor` | `bg_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Factor of the background ('bg_interpolated'). |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Percentile of the MIP below which values enter Otsu. |
| `MinThreshold` | `min_threshold` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest threshold. |
| `MaxThreshold` | `max_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Largest threshold (empty: none). |
| `BaseThreshold` | `base_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Base threshold weighed with the local ones (empty: none). |
| `volThrsh` | `vol_thrsh` | par |  | `1000` | `1000` | `@isnumeric` | Objects smaller than this are removed. |
| `offset` | `offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Added to the input's nonzero voxels. |
| `localWinSize` | `local_win_size` | par | size_yxz | `[15,15,23]` | `(23, 15, 15)` | `@isvector` | Window (z, y, x) of the point detection. |
| `SigmaThrsh` | `sigma_thrsh` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma thresholds of the point detection (one pass each). |
| `intThrsh` | `int_thrsh` | par |  | `10000` | `10000` | `@isnumeric` | Intensity thresholds of the point detection. |
| `initDetect` | `init_detect` | par |  | `'2d'` | `'2d'` | `@ischar` | Initial detection: '2d' (z MIP) or '3d' (the volume). |
| `detVolThrsh` | `det_vol_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest punctum volumes of the point detection. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to process. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `Save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as uint16 (else single). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the batches as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes batches too. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemovalPointDetection` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_point_detection`

``XR_ExM_PunctaRemovalPointDetection``: bright puncta of ``vol`` (a ``(z, y, x)`` single or double volume) found as peaks of its Gaussian filter (local maxima of the z MIP, or of the volume with ``'3d'``) above their windows' median by ``sigma_thrsh`` sigmas, each segmented around its peak (a local Otsu threshold raised until the region has at most ``det_vol_thrsh`` voxels), large regions dropped, and the rest filled slice by slice by linear interpolation of the pixels around them. Returns a new volume.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vol` | `vol` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Volume (z, y, x). |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `localWinSize` | `local_win_size` | par | size_yxz | `[41,41,41]` | `(41, 41, 41)` | `@isvector` | Window (z, y, x) around a peak for its local statistics and its segmentation. |
| `SigmaThrsh` | `sigma_thrsh` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isvector` | A peak is kept above its background by this many sigmas. |
| `intThrsh` | `int_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isvector` | Peaks below this intensity are not removed. |
| `initDetect` | `init_detect` | par |  | `'2d'` | `'2d'` | `@ischar` | Initial detection: '2d' (z MIP) or '3d' (the volume). |
| `detVolThrsh` | `det_vol_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest volume of a punctum's region. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the Gaussian filter (None: all). (Python only) |
