### `XR_generate_image_list_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.stitch.imagelist_generate.generate_image_list`

Generate the image list (stage coordinates of the tiles) of datasets as ``ImageList_from_<method>.csv`` in their folder (the first folder for tile_position and tile_list).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one, or several with the same settings). |
| `generationMethod` | `generation_method` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | 'encoder', 'sqlite', 'tile_position' or 'tile_list': from encoder positions, the acquisition database, the overlap between neighboring tiles, or the given tile names, indices and interval. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the included channels. |
| `mapTilename` | `map_tilename` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Map tile names to standard ones (sqlite only). |
| `tilePatterns` | `tile_patterns` | par |  | `{'0000t','ch0','000x','000y','000z'}` | `('0000t', 'ch0', '000x', '000y', '000z')` | `@iscell` | Patterns for time, channel, x, y and z in the tile names ([a-zA-Z]*[0-9]* or [0-9]*[a-zA-Z]*). |
| `tileFilenames` | `tile_filenames` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Tile file names (tile_list). |
| `tileIndices` | `tile_indices` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| (isnumeric(x) && size(x, 2) == 5)` | Tile indices of the tile files, #file x 5, order t, c, x, y, z (tile_list). |
| `tileInterval` | `tile_interval` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| (isnumeric(x) && size(x, 1) == 1 && size(x, 2) == 3)` | Interval between neighboring tiles in um, order x, y, z (tile_list). |
| `DS` | `ds` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Data is in deskewed space. |

### `XR_LSFlatFieldCorrection` -> `petakit5d.flatfield.correction.flatfield_correct_volume`

Flat-field correction of light-sheet data (``XR_LSFlatFieldCorrection``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Rawdata` | `rawdata` | req | volume |  |  |  | Data, (z, y, x) or (y, x). |
| `LSImage` | `ls_image` | req | volume |  |  |  | Flat-field image (y, x), or a stack of them (z, y, x). |
| `background` | `background` | req | volume |  |  |  | Camera background image (y, x). |
| `LowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | `@isnumeric` | Lowest value of the normalized flat field. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | If set, added after the correction instead of the background. |
| `removeFFImBackground` | `remove_ff_im_background` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Subtract the background from the flat-field image. |
| `LSRescale` | `ls_rescale` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Divide the flat field by its maximum. |
| `castDataType` | `cast_data_type` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Cast the result to the data's class; otherwise single. |
| `out` | `out` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Array to write the result into (reused over frames). (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the correction (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `XR_matlab_stitching_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.stitch.dataset.stitch_dataset`

Stitch every volume of the image list(s) (XR_matlab_stitching_wrapper): see the module. Sizes and per-axis values in (z, y, x), boxes 0-based and end-exclusive.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Folder(s) of the tiles (one per group with multiLoc). |

| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'matlab_stitch'` | `'matlab_stitch'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under the (first) data folder. |

| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the tiles to stitch. |

| `DS` | `ds` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles deskewed. |
| `DSR` | `dsr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles deskewed and rotated (stage coordinates). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse the z axis. |

| `axisOrder` | `axis_order` | par | same | `'x,y,z'` | `'x,y,z'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the stage coordinates ('x,y,z', '-x,y,z', ...). |

| `IOScan` | `io_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Inverted objective scan (stage coordinates, DSR space). |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large tiles (registration and maps on MIP slabs). |

| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) of the results (Zarr v3). |
| `resampleType` | `resample_type` | par | same | `'xy_isotropic'` | `'xy_isotropic'` | `@ischar` | 'given', 'isotropic' or 'xy_isotropic'. |

| `xcorrShift` | `xcorr_shift` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Register the tiles. |
| `xyMaxOffset` | `xy_max_offset` | par | same | `300` | `300` | `@(x) isvector(x) && numel(x) <= 2` | Largest shift in x and y (voxels). |

| `outBbox` | `out_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && (isempty(x) \|\| all(size(x) == [3, 2]) \|\| numel(x) == 6)` | Box of the stitched volume kept (None ends: to the end). |

| `shiftMethod` | `shift_method` | par |  | `'grid'` | `'grid'` | `@ischar` | 'grid', 'local', 'global' or 'group'. |
| `axisWeight` | `axis_weight` | par | size_yxz | `[1,0.1,10]` | `(10, 1, 0.1)` | `@isnumeric` | Weights of the tile index axes (z, y, x) in the assignment. |
| `groupFile` | `group_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Groups of tiles (csv) for 'group'. |
| `primaryCh` | `primary_ch` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | The primary channel ('Cam<c>_ch<n>' or 'ch<n>'; '': the first found). |
| `usePrimaryCoords` | `use_primary_coords` | par | same | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Secondaries take the primary's stage coordinates. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the results as uint16. |
| `edgeArtifacts` | `edge_artifacts` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Edge erosion of the tiles (voxels; 0: none). |
| `distBboxes` | `dist_bboxes` | par | bboxes | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Boxes of the feather distance maps. |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the z MIPs of the results. |
| `stitchMIP` | `stitch_mip` | par | flags_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x)  \|\| (islogical(x) && (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 3))` | Stitch the tiles' MIPs (flags z, y, x; one value: z). |

| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Stitch the volumes in Slurm jobs. |

### `XR_microscopeAutomaticProcessing` -> `petakit5d.pipeline.automatic.automatic_processing`

Deskew/rotate and stitch the datasets' files (XR_microscopeAutomaticProcessing): see the module. Python values: boxes (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1), resample factors (z, y, x), axis orders 'zyx', MIP flags (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folder(s) of the same settings. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par | same | `false` | `False` | `@(x) (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 3) && islogical(x)` | Remove the results first (one value, or DS, DSR and stitch). |
| `streaming` | `streaming` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Real-time processing: wait for files still to come. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan step (um). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isscalar` | Pixel size (um). |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Reverse the z axis. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `zStageScan` | `z_stage_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Z stage scan. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `[true,true]` | `(True, True)` | `@(x) (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 2) && islogical(x)` | uint16 results of deskew/rotation and of stitching. |
| `dzFromEncoder` | `dz_from_encoder` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Estimate dz from the encoder positions. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the results as zarr. |
| `save3DStack` | `save_3d_stack` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the DS and DSR volumes. |
| `deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deskew the files. |
| `rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Rotate the deskewed files. |
| `stitch` | `stitch` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Stitch the deskewed and rotated files. |
| `parseSettingFile` | `parse_setting_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip z stacks after their setting files. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the z stacks. |
| `inputAxisOrder` | `input_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the input. |
| `outputAxisOrder` | `output_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the output. |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Combined deskew and rotation. |
| `FFCorrection` | `ff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flat-field correction. |
| `BKRemoval` | `bk_removal` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Background removal in the flat-field correction. |
| `lowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | `@isnumeric` | Lowest value of the normalized flat field. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Constant offset after the flat field (None: the background). |

| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Resampling (z, y, x) of the deskew (None: by resample_type). |

| `xcorrMode` | `xcorr_mode` | par |  | `'primaryFirst'` | `'primaryFirst'` | `@(x) ismember(lower(x), {'primary', 'primaryfirst', 'all'})` | 'primaryFirst', 'primary' or 'all'. |
| `xyMaxOffset` | `xy_max_offset` | par | same | `300` | `300` | `@isnumeric` | Largest shift in x and y (voxels). |

### `XR_MIP_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_dataset`

The MIPs of every file of the datasets that matches a channel pattern (``XR_MIP_wrapper``), as ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif``: tiff files (``save_mip_tiff``), zarr files (``save_mip_zarr``) or large zarr files (``mip_zarr``, each file's task runs its batches as jobs), one task per file through the job framework, uint16 (or single without ``save_16bit``). parameters.mat and .json of the call go into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'MIPs'` | `'MIPs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0','CamB_ch1'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0', 'CamB_ch1')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to project. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr files: MIPs in batches (mip_zarr). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[2048,2048,2048]` | `(2048, 2048, 2048)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | uint16 MIPs (else single). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the files on a local process pool first. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isscalar` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs/'` | `'../job_logs/'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_MIP_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_zarr`

MIPs of a large zarr file (``XR_MIP_zarr``): the MIPs of its batches along all three axes (``mip_block`` tasks through the job framework) into the MIP slabs ``<result_dir_name>/<name>_{y,x,z}.zarr``, then the MIPs ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` of the flagged axes from the slabs. With the flags of the block tasks that wrote the zarr (and no box or pool size) the slabs are merged from the MIPs of their blocks instead of reading the zarr. Existing MIPs (or slabs, with ``mip_slab``) are kept; existing slabs give the MIPs; a slab another writer published is kept. The slabs are published before the MIPs.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Zarr file. |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'MIPs'` | `'MIPs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder next to the zarr. |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[1,1,1]` | `(1, 1, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[2048,2048,2048]` | `(2048, 2048, 2048)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pool sizes (z, y, x) for the slabs: along the axis[, across it[, resampling]]. |
| `mipSlab` | `mip_slab` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Only the MIP slabs (no MIP tiffs). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the batches as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the batches on a local process pool. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs/'` | `'../job_logs/'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes batches too. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `blockFlagFullpaths` | `block_flag_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Flags of the block tasks that wrote the zarr, with the MIPs of their blocks. |

### `XR_resave_zarr_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.io.resave.resave_zarr_dataset`

Resave the zarr files of datasets that match a channel pattern (``XR_resave_zarr_wrapper``) as ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.zarr``, one ``resave_zarr`` task per file through the job framework. parameters.mat and .json of the call go into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'resaved_zarr'` | `'resaved_zarr'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[512,512,512]` | `(512, 512, 512)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) of a block task. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the output. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Not used (reserved). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'.zarr'}` | `('.zarr',)` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to resave. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to resave (None: all). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files and batches as Slurm jobs. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Resave in batches (else whole files when they are few). |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary results (empty: a new one). |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_RLdeconFrame3D` -> `petakit5d.decon.frame.decon_file`

| `zEdgeSoften` | `z_edge_soften` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |

### `XR_rotate_PSF` -> `petakit5d.decon.psf.rotate_psf`

Rotate a PSF file, as for rotated sample-scan data (``XR_rotate_PSF``): the PSF as double, rotated by ``rotate_volume`` (``zAniso = sin(angle) dz / px``, ``dz / px`` for objective scans), its zero voxels set to the median of its positive values below their ``high_prctile`` percentile, written to ``<folder>/Rotated/<name>.tif`` (uint16, or single without ``save_16bit``; first as ``<name>_<uuid>.tif``, then renamed) with parameters.mat (v7.3) and parameters.json of the call there; a file name without a folder is in the current folder (``./Rotated/``). An existing result is kept unless ``overwrite``.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PSFfile` | `psf_file` | req |  |  |  |  | The PSF tiff file. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Rotate again where the result exists. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan (zAniso dz / px). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isscalar` | Pixel size. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isscalar` | Scan step. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `HighPrctile` | `high_prctile` | par |  | `99` | `99` | `@isscalar` | Percentile below which positive values give the fill. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `Save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save uint16 (else single). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@isstr` | Id in the temporary name (empty: a new one). |

### `XR_stitching_frame_zarr_dev_v1` -> `petakit5d.stitch.volume.stitch_volume`

Stitch one volume from its tiles (``XR_stitching_frame_zarr_dev_v1``; the steps and files are in the module's docstring): the tiles (``tile_fullpaths``, at stage ``coordinates``) are prepared, registered (a primary channel with ``xcorr_shift``) or placed with a primary channel's shifts (``is_primary_ch`` False, ``stitch_info_fullpath``), blended by ``blend_method`` in batches and published as ``result_path`` (by default ``<data>/matlab_stitch/<name>.zarr``, the first tile's name without its last 21 characters), with its z MIP in ``MIPs/``. An existing result is kept.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tileFullpaths` | `tile_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@iscell` | The tiles (tiff or zarr files; raw names with processed tiles). |
| `coordinates` | `coordinates` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Stage coordinates (x, y, z, um), one row per tile. |
| `ResultPath` | `result_path` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | The stitched zarr ('': <data>/matlab_stitch/<name>.zarr). |
| `tileInfoFullpath` | `tile_info_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | A .mat file with the tiles, coordinates, tile indices and flips. |
| `stitchInfoDir` | `stitch_info_dir` | par |  | `'stitchInfo'` | `'stitchInfo'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the stitch information in the result folder. |
| `stitchInfoFullpath` | `stitch_info_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | The primary channel's stitch information (secondaries). |
| `ProcessedDirstr` | `processed_dirstr` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Folder of processed tiles in the data folder (e.g. 'DSR'). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `axisOrder` | `axis_order` | par | same | `'x,y,z'` | `'x,y,z'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the stage coordinates ('x,y,z', '-x,y,z', ...). |
| `dataOrder` | `data_order` | par | data_order | `'y,x,z'` | `'z,y,x'` | `@ischar` | Axes of the tile arrays ('z,y,x'). |
| `flippedTile` | `flipped_tile` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| all(islogical(x) \| isnumeric(x))` | Per tile: flipped (bidirectional scan). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan step (um). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isscalar` | Pixel size (um). |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `IOScan` | `io_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Scan of the sample stage along the objective (no deskew). |
| `sCMOSCameraFlip` | `s_cmos_camera_flip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `InputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | custom:_bbox_rows_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box of each tile read (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) (or one per tile location). |
| `tileOutBbox` | `tile_out_bbox` | par | custom:_bbox_rows_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box of each tile kept after the flip (or one per tile location). |
| `TileOffset` | `tile_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Counts added to the tiles (skewed space, edge erosion). |
| `df` | `df` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the result as uint16. |
| `EdgeArtifacts` | `edge_artifacts` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Edge erosion of the tiles (voxels; 0: none). |
| `Decon` | `decon` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `DS` | `ds` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles deskewed. |
| `DSR` | `dsr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles deskewed and rotated. |
| `resampleType` | `resample_type` | par | same | `'xy_isotropic'` | `'xy_isotropic'` | `@ischar` | 'xy_isotropic', 'isotropic', 'method_1' or 'given'. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Resampling of the tiles (z, y, x), in the tiles' axis order (None: none). |
| `deconRotate` | `decon_rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `BlendMethod` | `blend_method` | par |  | `'none'` | `'none'` | `@ischar` | 'none', 'feather', 'max', 'mean', 'median' or 'separate'. |
| `blendWeightDegree` | `blend_weight_degree` | par |  | `10` | `10` | `@isnumeric` | Power of the feather weights. |
| `halfOrder` | `half_order` | par | custom:_half_order_in | `[3,2,1]` | `(0, 1, 2)` | `@isnumeric` | Order of the axes (z, y, x) in which overlaps are split. |
| `overlapType` | `overlap_type` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | '' (by the blend method), 'none', 'half', 'full'. |
| `xcorrShift` | `xcorr_shift` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Register the tiles. |
| `isPrimaryCh` | `is_primary_ch` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Primary channel (registered); else the primary's information. |
| `usePrimaryCoords` | `use_primary_coords` | par | same | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | A secondary takes the primary's stage coordinates. |
| `stitchPadSize` | `stitch_pad_size` | par | size_yxz | `[2,2,2]` | `(2, 2, 2)` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && numel(x) == 3` | Padding per tile along each axis (z, y, x) with registration. |
| `outBbox` | `out_bbox` | par | custom:_out_bbox_in | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && (isempty(x) \|\| all(size(x) == [3, 2]) \|\| numel(x) == 6)` | Box of the stitched volume kept (None ends: to the end). |
| `zNormalize` | `z_normalize` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `xcorrDownsample` | `xcorr_downsample` | par | size_yxz | `[2,2,1]` | `(1, 2, 2)` | `@isnumeric` | Downsampling of the registration (z, y, x). |
| `xcorrThresh` | `xcorr_thresh` | par |  | `0.25` | `0.25` | `@isnumeric` | Pairs below this correlation are not used. |
| `xyMaxOffset` | `xy_max_offset` | par |  | `300` | `300` | `@isnumeric` | Largest shift in x and y (voxels). |
| `zMaxOffset` | `z_max_offset` | par |  | `50` | `50` | `@isnumeric` | Largest shift in z (voxels). |
| `shiftMethod` | `shift_method` | par |  | `'grid'` | `'grid'` | `@ischar` | 'grid', 'local', 'global', 'group' or 'test'. |
| `axisWeight` | `axis_weight` | par | size_yxz | `[1,0.1,10]` | `(10, 1, 0.1)` | `@isnumeric` | Weights of the tile index axes (z, y, x) in the assignment. |
| `groupFile` | `group_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Groups of tiles (csv) for 'group'. |
| `singleDistMap` | `single_dist_map` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | One distance map for the tiles of a location. |
| `distBboxes` | `dist_bboxes` | par | bboxes | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Boxes of the distance maps. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Tiles are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large tiles (registration and maps on MIP slabs). |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pooling of the MIP slabs (z, y, x) for large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size of the result (z, y, x). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size of the blending (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) (Zarr v3). |

| `resLevel` | `res_level` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | Not ported (levels of the multi-resolution result). |
| `BorderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[0,0,0]` | `(0, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Border of the blending batches (z, y, x). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the z MIP of the result. |
| `tileIdx` | `tile_idx` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Tile indices (x, y, z, location), one row per tile. |

### `XR_tiffToZarr_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.io.convert.tiff_to_zarr_dataset`

Convert the tiff files of datasets (those that match a channel pattern) to zarr (``XR_tiffToZarr_wrapper``): ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.zarr``, one ``tiff_to_zarr`` task per file through the job framework, with per-file flips, per-location boxes and per-channel user functions (picked by keywords in the file names). parameters.mat and .json of the call go into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `tiffFullpaths` | `tiff_fullpaths` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Tiff files (or a folder of them) when data_paths is empty. |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'zarr'` | `'zarr'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `locIds` | `loc_ids` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Location ids of the tiles (stitching). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) (Zarr v3 with shards). |
| `flippedTile` | `flipped_tile` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Per file: flip it in z. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling factors (z, y, x) or (z, xy) (None: none). |
| `partialFile` | `partial_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Join each file's partial files (*_part[0-9]{3}.tif). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'tif'}` | `('tif',)` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to convert. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | custom:_bbox_rows_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to read (or one per tile location). |
| `tileOutBbox` | `tile_out_bbox` | par | custom:_bbox_rows_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to keep (or one per tile location). |
| `readWholeTiff` | `read_whole_tiff` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Read each tiff at once (else by z blocks). |
| `processFunPath` | `process_fun_path` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | User function files (.mat or .txt) per channel. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `bigData` | `big_data` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Big dataset (less memory per task). |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Not used. |
| `maxTrialNum` | `max_trial_num` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | Not used (two tries). |
| `unitWaitTime` | `unit_wait_time` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_unmix_channels_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_dataset`

Unmix a channel of every time point of the datasets (``XR_unmix_channels_data_wrapper``): the files are grouped by channel (``channel_patterns``, in each folder, or over the folders when each holds one channel), groups without every channel are left out (a warning), and each group is one ``unmix_channels_file`` task (``unmix_channels_zarr`` with ``large_file``) through the job framework, its result in ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/``.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@ischar` | 'linear', or 'gaussian' (each channel filtered with its sigma). |
| `unmixSigmas` | `unmix_sigmas` | par | custom:_sigmas_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Sigmas of the gaussian mode, one per channel: a scalar or (z, y, x). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Unmixed'` | `'Unmixed'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA','CamB'}` | `('CamA', 'CamB')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files. |
| `channelInd` | `channel_ind` | par | index1 | `1` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | The channel to unmix (0-based). |
| `FFCorrection` | `ff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flat-field correct the channels first (not large files). |
| `lowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | `@isnumeric` | Lowest value of the normalized flat fields. |
| `FFImagePaths` | `ff_image_paths` | par |  | `{'',''}` | `('', '')` | `@iscell` | Flat-field image of each channel. |
| `backgroundPaths` | `background_paths` | par |  | `{'',''}` | `('', '')` | `@iscell` | Background image of each channel. |
| `constBackground` | `const_background` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background (scalar, or one per channel) subtracted first. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | If set, added after the unmixing. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr files: unmix in blocks (unmix_channels_zarr). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the results as zarr (large files: always). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save uint16; otherwise single. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `borderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[0,0,0]` | `(0, 0, 0)` | `@isvector` | Border (z, y, x) of the batches of large files. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the frames as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `XR_unmix_channels_frame` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_file`

Unmix one frame (``XR_unmix_channels_frame``): the channels' files (one per channel, tiff or zarr with ``zarr_file``) into ``<data path of channel channel_ind>/ <result_dir_name>/<its name>.tif`` (or ``.zarr``, chunks ``block_size``), uint16 with ``save_16bit`` else single. Channels with factor 0 are not read. Nothing is done if the result exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpaths` | `frame_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Files of the channels (tiff or zarr). |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@ischar` | 'linear', or 'gaussian' (each channel filtered with its sigma). |
| `unmixSigmas` | `unmix_sigmas` | par | custom:_sigmas_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Sigmas of the gaussian mode, one per channel: a scalar or (z, y, x). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Unmixed'` | `'Unmixed'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under the data path. |
| `channelInd` | `channel_ind` | par | index1 | `1` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | The channel to unmix (0-based). |
| `FFCorrection` | `ff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flat-field correct the channels first. |
| `lowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | `@isnumeric` | Lowest value of the normalized flat fields. |
| `FFImagePaths` | `ff_image_paths` | par |  | `{'','',''}` | `('', '', '')` | `@iscell` | Flat-field image of each channel. |
| `backgroundPaths` | `background_paths` | par |  | `{'','',''}` | `('', '', '')` | `@iscell` | Background image of each channel. |
| `constBackground` | `const_background` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background of each channel, subtracted first. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | If set, added after the unmixing. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save uint16; otherwise single. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The channels are zarr. |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as zarr. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of a zarr result. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `XR_unmix_channels_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_zarr`

Unmix large zarr files in blocks (``XR_unmix_channels_zarr``): the batches of the image (``batch_size``, whole chunks of ``block_size``, with ``border_size`` in 'gaussian' mode) as ``unmix_channels_block`` or ``unmix_channels_gaussian_block`` tasks through the job framework into ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>_<uuid>.zarr``, then moved into place as ``<name>.zarr``, in the class of the channel ``channel_ind``. Nothing is done if the result exists; a result another writer published is kept. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpaths` | `zarr_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Zarr files of the channels. |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@ischar` | 'linear', or 'gaussian' (each channel filtered with its sigma). |
| `unmixSigmas` | `unmix_sigmas` | par | custom:_sigmas_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Sigmas of the gaussian mode, one per channel: a scalar or (z, y, x). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Unmixed'` | `'Unmixed'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under the data path. |
| `channelInd` | `channel_ind` | par | index1 | `1` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | The channel to unmix (0-based). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used (the result has the channel's class). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of a block. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `borderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[0,0,0]` | `(0, 0, 0)` | `@isvector` | Border (z, y, x) of each batch. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks on a local process pool. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `XR_zarrToTiff_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.io.convert.zarr_to_tiff_dataset`

Convert the zarr files of datasets (``<data path>/*.zarr`` that contain a channel pattern) to ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.tif`` (``XR_zarrToTiff_wrapper``), one ``zarr_to_tiff`` task per file through the job framework.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'tiffs'` | `'tiffs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to convert. |
| `usrFcn` | `usr_fcn` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x)` | User function applied to each volume (import path or record). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Not used. |
| `maxTrialNum` | `max_trial_num` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | Not used (two tries, then one). |
| `unitWaitTime` | `unit_wait_time` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `zarrToTiff` -> `petakit5d.io.convert.zarr_to_tiff`

Convert a zarr array (the port's or MATLAB's layout) to a tiff file (``zarrToTiff``), after the user function if one is given. An existing result is kept (``overwrite``: replaced first).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFilename` | `zarr_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Zarr file (either layout). |
| `tiffFilename` | `tiff_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Tiff result. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `usrFcn` | `usr_fcn` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x)` | User function applied to the volume (callable, import path or record). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary result (empty: a new one). |
